64		public static bool CanPlace(Point origin, StructureMap structures)
65		{
66			if (WorldGen.BiomeTileCheck(origin.X, origin.Y))
67			{
68				return false;
69			}
70			return !GenBase._tiles[origin.X, origin.Y].active();
71		}

73		public override bool Place(Point origin, StructureMap structures, GenerationProgress progress)
74		{
75			if (GenBase._tiles[origin.X, origin.Y].active())
76			{
77				return false;
78			}
79			origin.X -= _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0) / 2;
80			origin.Y -= _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1) / 2;
81			BuildMagmaMap(origin);
82			SimulatePressure(out var effectedMapArea);
83			PlaceGranite(origin, effectedMapArea);
84			CleanupTiles(origin, effectedMapArea);
85			PlaceDecorations(origin, effectedMapArea);
86			structures.AddStructure(effectedMapArea.Modified(origin.X, origin.Y, 0, 0), 8);
87			return true;
88		}

90		private void BuildMagmaMap(Point tileOrigin)
91		{
92			_sourceMagmaMap = new Magma[200, 200];
93			_targetMagmaMap = new Magma[200, 200];
94			for (int i = 0; i < _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0); i++)
95			{
96				for (int j = 0; j < _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1); j++)
97				{
98					int i2 = i + tileOrigin.X;
99					int j2 = j + tileOrigin.Y;
100					_sourceMagmaMap[i, j] = Magma.CreateEmpty((!WorldGen.SolidTile(i2, j2)) ? 1 : 4);
101					_targetMagmaMap[i, j] = _sourceMagmaMap[i, j];
102				}
103			}
104		}

106		private void SimulatePressure(out Rectangle effectedMapArea)
107		{
108			//IL_0072: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
109			//IL_0077: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
110			//IL_00a4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
111			//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

114			//IL_015d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
115			//IL_0162: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
116			//IL_0167: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
117			int length = _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0);
118			int length2 = _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1);
119			int num = length / 2;
120			int num2 = length2 / 2;
121			int num3 = num;
122			int num4 = num3;
123			int num5 = num2;
124			int num6 = num5;
125			for (int i = 0; i < 300; i++)
126			{
127				for (int j = num3; j <= num4; j++)
128				{
129					for (int k = num5; k <= num6; k++)
130					{
131						Magma magma = _sourceMagmaMap[j, k];
132						if (!magma.IsActive)
133						{
134							continue;
135						}
136						double num7 = 0.0;
137						Vector2D val = Vector2D.Zero;
138						for (int l = -1; l <= 1; l++)
139						{
140							for (int m = -1; m <= 1; m++)
141							{
142								if (l == 0 && m == 0)
143								{
144									continue;
145								}
146								Vector2D val2 = _normalisedVectors[(l + 1) * 3 + (m + 1)];
147								Magma magma2 = _sourceMagmaMap[j + l, k + m];
148								if (magma.Pressure > 0.01 && !magma2.IsActive)
149								{
150									if (l == -1)
151									{
152										num3 = Utils.Clamp(j + l, 1, num3);
153									}
154									else
155									{
156										num4 = Utils.Clamp(j + l, num4, length - 2);
157									}
158									if (m == -1)
159									{
160										num5 = Utils.Clamp(k + m, 1, num5);
161									}
162									else
163									{
164										num6 = Utils.Clamp(k + m, num6, length2 - 2);
165									}
166									_targetMagmaMap[j + l, k + m] = magma2.ToFlow();
167								}
168								double pressure = magma2.Pressure;
169								num7 += pressure;
170								val += pressure * val2;
171							}
172						}
173						num7 /= 8.0;
174						if (num7 > magma.Resistance)
175						{
176							double num8 = ((Vector2D)(ref val)).Length() / 8.0;
177							double val3 = Math.Max(num7 - num8 - magma.Pressure, 0.0) + num8 + magma.Pressure * 0.875 - magma.Resistance;
178							val3 = Math.Max(0.0, val3);
179							_targetMagmaMap[j, k] = Magma.CreateFlow(val3, Math.Max(0.0, magma.Resistance - val3 * 0.02));
180						}
181					}
182				}
183				if (i < 2)
184				{
185					_targetMagmaMap[num, num2] = Magma.CreateFlow(25.0);
186				}
187				Utils.Swap(ref _sourceMagmaMap, ref _targetMagmaMap);
188			}
189			effectedMapArea = new Rectangle(num3, num5, num4 - num3 + 1, num6 - num5 + 1);
190		}

192		private bool ShouldUseLava(Point tileOrigin)
193		{
194			int length = _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(0);
195			int length2 = _sourceMagmaMap.GetLength(1);
196			int num = length / 2;
197			int num2 = length2 / 2;
198			if (tileOrigin.Y + num2 <= GenVars.lavaLine - 30)
199			{
200				return false;
201			}
202			for (int i = -50; i < 50; i++)
203			{
204				for (int j = -50; j < 50; j++)
205				{
206					if (WorldGen.InWorld(tileOrigin.X + num + i, tileOrigin.Y + num2 + j, 10) && GenBase._tiles[tileOrigin.X + num + i, tileOrigin.Y + num2 + j].active())
207					{
208						ushort type = GenBase._tiles[tileOrigin.X + num + i, tileOrigin.Y + num2 + j].type;
209						if (type == 147 || (uint)(type - 161) <= 2u || type == 200)
210						{
211							return false;
212						}
213					}
214				}
215			}
216			return true;
217		}

219		private void PlaceGranite(Point tileOrigin, Rectangle magmaMapArea)
220		{
221			bool flag = ShouldUseLava(tileOrigin);
222			ushort type = 368;
223			ushort wall = 180;
224			if (WorldGen.drunkWorldGen)
225			{
226				type = 367;
227				wall = 178;
228			}
229			for (int i = magmaMapArea.Left; i < magmaMapArea.Right; i++)
230			{
231				for (int j = magmaMapArea.Top; j < magmaMapArea.Bottom; j++)
232				{
233					if (!WorldGen.InWorld(tileOrigin.X + i, tileOrigin.Y + j, 10))
234					{
235						continue;
236					}
237					Magma magma = _sourceMagmaMap[i, j];
238					if (!magma.IsActive)
239					{
240						continue;
241					}
242					Tile tile = GenBase._tiles[tileOrigin.X + i, tileOrigin.Y + j];
243					double num = Math.Sin((double)(tileOrigin.Y + j) * 0.4) * 0.7 + 1.2;
244					double num2 = 0.2 + 0.5 / Math.Sqrt(Math.Max(0.0, magma.Pressure - magma.Resistance));
245					if (Math.Max(1.0 - Math.Max(0.0, num * num2), magma.Pressure / 15.0) > 0.35 + (WorldGen.SolidTile(tileOrigin.X + i, tileOrigin.Y + j) ? 0.0 : 0.5))
246					{
247						if (TileID.Sets.Ore[tile.type])
248						{
249							tile.ResetToType(tile.type);
250						}
251						else
252						{
253							tile.ResetToType(type);
254						}
255						tile.wall = wall;
256					}
257					else if (magma.Resistance < 0.01)
258					{
259						WorldUtils.ClearTile(tileOrigin.X + i, tileOrigin.Y + j);
260						tile.wall = wall;
261					}
262					if (tile.liquid > 0 && flag)
263					{
264						tile.liquidType(1);
265					}
266				}
267			}
268		}
269	
270		private void CleanupTiles(Point tileOrigin, Rectangle magmaMapArea)
271		{
272			ushort wall = 180;
273			if (WorldGen.drunkWorldGen)
274			{
275				wall = 178;
276			}
277			List<Point16> list = new List<Point16>();
278			for (int i = magmaMapArea.Left; i < magmaMapArea.Right; i++)
279			{
280				for (int j = magmaMapArea.Top; j < magmaMapArea.Bottom; j++)
281				{
282					if (!_sourceMagmaMap[i, j].IsActive)
283					{
284						continue;
285					}
286					int num = 0;
287					int num2 = i + tileOrigin.X;
288					int num3 = j + tileOrigin.Y;
289					if (!WorldGen.InWorld(num2, num3, 10) || !WorldGen.SolidTile(num2, num3))
290					{
291						continue;
292					}
293					for (int k = -1; k <= 1; k++)
294					{
295						for (int l = -1; l <= 1; l++)
296						{
297							if (WorldGen.SolidTile(num2 + k, num3 + l))
298							{
299								num++;
300							}
301						}
302					}
303					if (num < 3)
304					{
305						list.Add(new Point16(num2, num3));
306					}
307				}
308			}
309			foreach (Point16 item in list)
310			{
311				int x = item.X;
312				int y = item.Y;
313				WorldUtils.ClearTile(x, y, frameNeighbors: true);
314				GenBase._tiles[x, y].wall = wall;
315			}
316			list.Clear();
317		}
318	
319		private void PlaceDecorations(Point tileOrigin, Rectangle magmaMapArea)
320		{
321			FastRandom fastRandom = new FastRandom(Main.ActiveWorldFileData.Seed).WithModifier(65440uL);
322			for (int i = magmaMapArea.Left; i < magmaMapArea.Right; i++)
323			{
324				for (int j = magmaMapArea.Top; j < magmaMapArea.Bottom; j++)
325				{
326					Magma magma = _sourceMagmaMap[i, j];
327					int num = i + tileOrigin.X;
328					int num2 = j + tileOrigin.Y;
329					if (!WorldGen.InWorld(num, num2, 10) || !magma.IsActive)
330					{
331						continue;
332					}
333					WorldUtils.TileFrame(num, num2);
334					WorldGen.SquareWallFrame(num, num2);
335					FastRandom fastRandom2 = fastRandom.WithModifier(num, num2);
336					if (fastRandom2.Next(8) == 0 && GenBase._tiles[num, num2].active())
337					{
338						if (!GenBase._tiles[num, num2 + 1].active())
339						{
340							WorldGen.PlaceUncheckedStalactite(num, num2 + 1, fastRandom2.Next(2) == 0, fastRandom2.Next(3), spiders: false);
341						}
342						if (!GenBase._tiles[num, num2 - 1].active())
343						{
344							WorldGen.PlaceUncheckedStalactite(num, num2 - 1, fastRandom2.Next(2) == 0, fastRandom2.Next(3), spiders: false);
345						}
346					}
347					if (fastRandom2.Next(2) == 0)
348					{
349						Tile.SmoothSlope(num, num2);
350					}
351				}
352			}
353		}
354	}
355

- **Granite** `PlaceDecorations` 用 `FastRandom(Seed).WithModifier(65440)`（:321）独立随机流 + `Next(8)==0` 石笋 + `Next(2)==0` 才 SmoothSlope（:347）——只对 50% 格子 smooth，且 smooth 之后**不再清 slope**，所以花岗岩区若 typ 差 483 vs 44，是 `PlaceUncheckedStalactite` 的 id 空间问题（44=石笋? 让我查）。

再读 JS 实现与 J 批工具。

110	    | ((solidOrSlopedAt(st, x - 1, y) ? 1 : 0) << 1)
111	    | (solidOrSlopedAt(st, x + 1, y) ? 1 : 0);
112	  const flag2 = aboveNotSolid && aboveNotEmpty;

115	    case 9: if (!flag2) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 4; } break;
116	    case 6: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 1; break;
117	    case 5: st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 2; break;
118	    case 4: st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 1; break;

123	/** TileID.Sets.Ore（TileID.cs:399，19 个）——大理石/花岗岩 ResetToType 的矿石保留判定。
124	 *  曾抄成 20/203/70/21/116/234 的杂表 → 花岗岩体内矿石被吞（granite checkpoint 纯 type 差） */
125	const VANILLA_ORE = new Set([7, 166, 6, 167, 9, 168, 8, 169, 22, 204, 37, 58, 107, 221, 108, 222, 111, 223, 211]);
126	function isOreType(t: number): boolean {
127	  const v = TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet;
128	  return v != null && VANILLA_ORE.has(v);
129	}
130	
131	export function runMarbleGranitePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
132	  // Marble(12800)/Granite(12884) 各自是独立 GenPass——RunPass 每 pass 前重播种子
133	  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶Marble cs:12800
134	  runMarblePass(st, rng, gs);
135	  rng.reseed(gs.seed); // ▶Granite cs:12884
136	  runGranitePass(st, rng, gs);
137	}
138	
139	/** cs:12800-12883：采样带 = n/num*(w-200)+100 起宽 (w-160)/num；
140	 *  y∈[GenVars.rockLayer+20, +(h-rockLayer-40-200))；中部 45-55% 重掷 beachDistance..w-；
141	 *  100 次预算；失败计数 > w*10 收缩 count。Place 内联在采样环（BiomeTileCheck 双查）。 */
142	export function runMarblePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
143	  let count = worldGenRangeCount(st, rng, 4, 8, 'area');
144	  let placed = 0, fails = 0;
145	  while (placed < count) {
146	    const frac = placed / count;
147	    let x = 0, y = 0, ok = false;
148	    let budget = 100;
149	    while (!ok) {
150	      budget--;
151	      if (budget <= 0) break;
152	      const x0 = Math.trunc(frac * (st.w - 200)) + 100;
153	      x = rng.int(x0, x0 + Math.trunc((st.w - 160) / count) - 1);   // RandomRectanglePoint 先 X
154	      const y0 = Math.trunc(gs.genRockLayer) + 20;                   // GenVars.rockLayer（raw 截断）
155	      y = rng.int(y0, y0 + (st.h - (Math.trunc(gs.genRockLayer) + 40) - 200) - 1);  // 后 Y
156	      while (x > st.w * 0.45 && x < st.w * 0.55) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, st.w - gs.beachDistance - 1);
157	      ok = true;
158	    }
159	    fails++;
160	    if (ok && marblePlace(st, rng, x, y)) { placed++; fails = 0; }
161	    else if (fails > st.w * 10) { count = placed; placed++; fails = 0; }
162	  }
163	}
164	
165	/** MarbleBiome.Place（MarbleBiome.cs:180-253）1:1 */
166	function marblePlace(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ox0: number, oy0: number): boolean {
167	  if (biomeTileCheck(st, ox0, oy0)) return false;
168	  const bn = Math.trunc(rng.int(80, 149) / 3);          // Next(80,150)/3
169	  const bn2 = Math.trunc(rng.int(40, 59) / 3);          // Next(40,60)/3
170	  const bn3 = Math.trunc((bn2 * 3 - rng.int(20, 29)) / 3);
171	  const ox = ox0 - Math.trunc(bn * 3 / 2), oy = oy0 - Math.trunc(bn2 * 3 / 2);
172	  // Slab 状态机：0空 1实心 2半砖 3右下 4左下 5右上 6左上
173	  const state: number[][] = [];
174	  const hasWall: boolean[][] = [];
175	  for (let i = 0; i < bn + 2; i++) { state.push(new Array(bn2 + 2).fill(0)); hasWall.push(new Array(bn2 + 2).fill(false)); }
176	  const groupSolid = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
177	    let cnt = 0;
178	    for (let a = 0; a < 3; a++) for (let b = 0; b < 3; b++) if (solidOrSlopedAt(st, x + a, y + b)) cnt++;
179	    return cnt > 0;                                     // scale/4*3 = 0（int 除）；IsGroupSolid 用 SolidOrSlopedTile
180	  };
181	  for (let i = -1; i < bn + 1; i++) {
182	    const n4 = (i - Math.trunc(bn / 2)) / bn + 0.5;
183	    const n5 = Math.trunc((0.5 - Math.abs(n4 - 0.5)) * 5) - 2;
184	    for (let j = -1; j < bn2 + 1; j++) {
185	      let wall = true, solid = false;
186	      const g2 = groupSolid(i * 3 + ox, j * 3 + oy);
187	      const n6 = Math.abs(j - Math.trunc(bn2 / 2)) - Math.trunc(bn3 / 4) + n5;
188	      if (n6 > 3) { solid = g2; wall = false; }
189	      else if (n6 > 0) { solid = j - Math.trunc(bn2 / 2) > 0 || g2; wall = j - Math.trunc(bn2 / 2) < 0 || n6 <= 2; }
190	      else if (n6 === 0) solid = rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && (j - Math.trunc(bn2 / 2) > 0 || g2);
191	      if (Math.abs(n4 - 0.5) > 0.35 + rng.next() * 0.1 && !g2) { wall = false; solid = false; }
192	      state[i + 1]![j + 1] = solid ? 1 : 0;
193	      hasWall[i + 1]![j + 1] = wall;
194	    }
195	  }
196	  const slabSolid = (s: number, lx: number, ly: number, scale: number): boolean => {
197	    switch (s) {
198	      case 0: return false;
199	      case 1: return true;
200	      case 2: return ly >= Math.trunc(scale / 2);
201	      case 3: return lx >= scale - ly;
202	      case 4: return lx < ly;
203	      case 5: return lx > ly;
204	      case 6: return lx < scale - ly;
205	      default: return true;
206	    }
207	  };
208	  // SmoothSlope（MarbleBiome.cs:79-110）：4 邻实心码 → 状态重分类
209	  for (let i = 0; i < bn; i++) {
210	    for (let j = 0; j < bn2; j++) {
211	      const sx = i + 1, sy = j + 1;
212	      if (state[sx]![sy] === 0) continue;
213	      const code = ((state[sx]![sy - 1] !== 0 ? 1 : 0) << 3)
214	        | ((state[sx]![sy + 1] !== 0 ? 1 : 0) << 2)
215	        | ((state[sx - 1]![sy] !== 0 ? 1 : 0) << 1)
216	        | (state[sx + 1]![sy] !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
217	      state[sx]![sy] = code === 10 ? 6 : code === 9 ? 5 : code === 6 ? 4 : code === 5 ? 3 : code === 4 ? 2 : 1;
218	    }
219	  }
220	  const bn7 = Math.trunc(bn / 2), bn8 = Math.trunc(bn2 / 2);
221	  const bn9 = (bn8 + 1) * (bn8 + 1);
222	  const vv = rng.next() * 2 - 1, vv2 = rng.next() * 2 - 1, vv3 = rng.next() * 2 - 1;
223	  let n11 = 0;
224	  for (let m = 0; m <= bn; m++) {
225	    const n12 = bn8 / bn7 * (m - bn7);
226	    const n13 = Math.min(bn8, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, bn9 - n12 * n12))));
227	    n11 = m >= Math.trunc(bn / 2)
228	      ? n11 + (vv2 + (vv3 - vv2) * (m / Math.trunc(bn / 2) - 1))
229	      : n11 + (vv + (vv2 - vv) * (m / Math.trunc(bn / 2)));
230	    for (let n = bn8 - n13; n <= bn8 + n13; n++) {
231	      // PlaceSlab（scale=3）
232	      const pOX = m * 3 + ox, pOY = n * 3 + oy + Math.trunc(n11);
233	      const pst = state[m + 1]![n + 1]!, pWall = hasWall[m + 1]![n + 1]!;
234	      const __probe = (globalThis as { __swSlabProbe?: (x: number, y: number, pst: number, pWall: boolean) => void }).__swSlabProbe;
235	      if (__probe) __probe(pOX, pOY, pst, pWall);
236	      let pn4 = 0, pn5 = 3;
237	      for (let i = -1; i < 4; i++) {
238	        if ((i === -1 || i === 3) && rng.int(0, 1) === 0) continue;   // 边缘列 50% 跳过
239	        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) pn4--;
240	        if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) pn5++;
241	        for (let j = pn4; j < pn5; j++) {
242	          const px = pOX + i, py = pOY + j;
243	          if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
244	          const ti = st.idx(px, py);
245	          st.type[ti] = isOreType(st.type[ti]) ? st.type[ti] : MARBLE;  // ResetToType：矿石保留
246	          st.flags[ti] = slabSolid(pst, i, j, 3) ? 1 : 0;
247	          st.liquid[ti] = 0; st.liquidType[ti] = 0;
248	          st.half[ti] = 0; st.slope[ti] = 0;  // ResetToType 清 sTileHeader（半砖/坡面）
249	          // ★wall 是 Tile.cs 独立 ushort 字段——ResetToType 的 bTileHeader*=0 不清墙，
250	          // HasWall=false 时保留旧墙（SquareWallFrame 掷照旧）
251	          if (pWall) st.wall[ti] = 178;
252	          // WorldUtils.TileFrame(frameNeighbors:true)（cs:WorldUtils.cs:79-88）：
253	          // 中心 + (x+1,y)/(x-1,y)/(x,y+1)/(x,y-1) 各一次 TileFrameImportant——
254	          // 165 格 → CheckStalactite 重查（支撑被后续格上坡 → 击杀/重定型掷）
255	          frame165(st, rng, px, py);
256	          frame165(st, rng, px + 1, py);
257	          frame165(st, rng, px - 1, py);
258	          frame165(st, rng, px, py + 1);
259	          frame165(st, rng, px, py - 1);
260	          // SquareWallFrame：中心格 WallFrame(resetFrame:true) 非零墙掷 1×Next(0,3)
261	          //（PlaceSlab 每格,cs:MarbleBiome.cs:160-168）
262	          squareWallFrameRoll(st, rng, px, py);
263	          // Tile.SmoothSlope（applyToNeighbors=true）：无掷但写 half/slope——
264	          // 影响本列/邻列后续 SolidTile 门（PlaceTight 骰）与最终坡面
265	          tileSmoothSlope(st, px, py);
266	          if (solidTileAt(st, px, py - 1) && rng.int(0, 3) === 0) placeTight(st, rng, px, py);
267	          if (solidTileAt(st, px, py) && rng.int(0, 3) === 0) placeTight(st, rng, px, py - 1);
268	        }
269	      }
270	    }
271	  }
272	  return true;
273	}
274	
275	/** cs:12884-12975：采样与放置两段分离——先收点（CanPlace = !BiomeTileCheck && !active），
276	 *  再统一 Place。带宽 (w-200)/num。 */
277	export function runGranitePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
278	  let count = worldGenRangeCount(st, rng, 4, 8, 'width');
279	  const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];
280	  let placed = 0, fails = 0;
281	  while (placed < count) {
282	    const frac = placed / count;
283	    let x = 0, y = 0, ok = false;
284	    let budget = 100;
285	    while (!ok) {
286	      budget--;
287	      if (budget <= 0) break;
288	      const x0 = Math.trunc(frac * (st.w - 200)) + 100;
289	      x = rng.int(x0, x0 + Math.trunc((st.w - 200) / count) - 1);
290	      const y0 = Math.trunc(gs.genRockLayer) + 20;
291	      y = rng.int(y0, y0 + (st.h - (Math.trunc(gs.genRockLayer) + 40) - 200) - 1);
292	      while (x > st.w * 0.45 && x < st.w * 0.55) x = rng.int(gs.beachDistance, st.w - gs.beachDistance - 1);
293	      ok = true;
294	    }
295	    fails++;
296	    if (ok && !biomeTileCheck(st, x, y) && !(st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)])) {
297	      pts.push([x, y]); placed++;
298	    } else if (fails > st.w * 10) { count = placed; placed++; fails = 0; }
299	  }
300	  for (let i = 0; i < count && i < pts.length; i++) granitePlace(st, rng, gs, pts[i]![0]!, pts[i]![1]!);
301	}
302	
303	/** GraniteBiome.Place（GraniteBiome.cs:73-353）1:1：magma 压力 CA → 放置 → 清理 → 装饰 */
304	function granitePlace(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, x0: number, y0: number): void {
305	  if (!st.inBounds(x0, y0) || st.flags[st.idx(x0, y0)]) return;
306	  const gox = x0 - 100, goy = y0 - 100;             // 200×200 magma 图
307	  // let:轮末 Utils.Swap 引用交换(GraniteBiome.cs:186)
308	  let pres = new Float64Array(200 * 200);
309	  let res = new Float64Array(200 * 200);
310	  let mact = new Uint8Array(200 * 200);
311	  for (let a = 0; a < 200; a++) {
312	    for (let b = 0; b < 200; b++) {
313	      res[a * 200 + b] = solidTileAt(st, a + gox, b + goy) ? 4 : 1;
314	    }
315	  }
316	  // 8 邻域单位向量（Vector2D.Normalize）
317	  const NVX = [-Math.SQRT1_2, -1, -Math.SQRT1_2, 0, 0, 0, Math.SQRT1_2, 1, Math.SQRT1_2];
318	  const NVY = [-Math.SQRT1_2, 0, Math.SQRT1_2, -1, 0, 1, -Math.SQRT1_2, 0, Math.SQRT1_2];
319	  let x3 = 100, x4 = 100, y3 = 100, y4 = 100;
320	  // ★原版双缓冲稀疏写+轮末 Swap(GraniteBiome.cs:47-49/:186-187):target 在
321	  //  BuildMagmaMap 时全新(全 0),循环只写被激活/过压力门的格,轮末交换数组引用——
322	  //  本轮未写格保留 **i-2 轮**值。曾每轮 tPres.set(pres) 全量刷新 → 未写格保留
323	  //  i-1 轮值,CA 边界低压力格行为分叉
324	  // ★BuildMagmaMap(GraniteBiome.cs:96-107):target 缓冲初始为 source 的**别名拷贝**
325	  //  (未写格 Resistance 恒保持地形初值 1/4,非全 0)——曾 new 全 0 数组,CA 边界
326	  //  电阻场差一个量级(oracle 对账揪出;此前为对齐 JS 曾在 oracle 镜像 0 初值,现
327	  //  双侧回正原版语义)
328	  const tPres0 = Float64Array.from(pres);
329	  const tRes0 = Float64Array.from(res);
330	  const tAct0 = Uint8Array.from(mact);
331	  let tPres = tPres0, tRes = tRes0, tAct = tAct0;
332	  const clamp = (v: number, lo: number, hi: number) => Math.min(hi, Math.max(lo, v));
333	  for (let it = 0; it < 300; it++) {
334	    for (let j = x3; j <= x4; j++) {
335	      for (let k = y3; k <= y4; k++) {
336	        const ji = j * 200 + k;
337	        if (!mact[ji]) continue;
338	        let sum = 0, vx = 0, vy = 0;
339	        for (let l = -1; l <= 1; l++) {
340	          for (let m = -1; m <= 1; m++) {
341	            if (l === 0 && m === 0) continue;
342	            const vi = (l + 1) * 3 + (m + 1);
343	            const ni = (j + l) * 200 + (k + m);
344	            const np = pres[ni]!;
345	            if (pres[ji]! > 0.01 && !mact[ni]) {
346	              if (l === -1) x3 = clamp(j + l, 1, x3); else x4 = clamp(j + l, x4, 198);
347	              if (m === -1) y3 = clamp(k + m, 1, y3); else y4 = clamp(k + m, y4, 198);
348	              // ToFlow(:31):保压/保阻,置 active——稀疏写下须整格写入 target
349	              tPres[ni] = pres[ni]!; tRes[ni] = res[ni]!; tAct[ni] = 1;
350	            }
351	            sum += np;
352	            vx += np * NVX[vi]!; vy += np * NVY[vi]!;
353	          }
354	        }
355	        sum /= 8;
356	        if (sum > res[ji]!) {
357	          const len = Math.sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy) / 8;
358	          let nv = Math.max(sum - len - pres[ji]!, 0) + len + pres[ji]! * 0.875 - res[ji]!;
359	          nv = Math.max(0, nv);
360	          tPres[ji] = nv;
361	          tRes[ji] = Math.max(0, res[ji]! - nv * 0.02);
362	          // CreateFlow(:31)恒 active:true——整格写入含激活位。曾只写压力/电阻：
363	          // 稀疏交换语义下本格 target 残留 i-2 轮 inactive 态 → CA 细胞提前死亡、
364	          // 花岗岩腔边界比原版小一圈（wall 180 网大面积缺失）
365	          tAct[ji] = 1;
366	        }
367	      }
368	    }
369	    // CreateFlow(25.0) 默认 resistance=0(:34)——中心注入须清 tRes(曾残留初值
370	    // 1/4,中心压差差一个量级并外传全图)
371	    if (it < 2) { tPres[100 * 200 + 100] = 25; tRes[100 * 200 + 100] = 0; tAct[100 * 200 + 100] = 1; }
372	    // Utils.Swap(引用交换,零拷贝)
373	    let tp = pres; pres = tPres; tPres = tp;
374	    tp = res; res = tRes; tRes = tp;
375	    let tq = mact; mact = tAct; tAct = tq;
376	  }
377	  // ShouldUseLava：中心线 ≤ lavaLine-30 免岩浆；±50 内有冰雪/薄冰(161-163)/200 拒绝
378	  const useLava = (() => {
379	    if (goy + 100 <= gs.lavaLine - 30) return false;
380	    for (let a = -50; a < 50; a++) {
381	      for (let b = -50; b < 50; b++) {
382	        const ax = gox + 100 + a, ay = goy + 100 + b;
383	        if (ax >= 10 && ax < st.w - 10 && ay >= 10 && ay < st.h - 10 && st.flags[st.idx(ax, ay)]) {
384	          const t = TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(ax, ay)]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? 0;
385	          if (t === 147 || t === 200 || (t >= 161 && t <= 163)) return false;
386	        }
387	      }
388	    }
389	    return true;
390	  })();
391	  // PlaceGranite
392	  for (let i = x3; i <= x4; i++) {
393	    for (let j = y3; j <= y4; j++) {
394	      const mi = i * 200 + j;
395	      const tx = gox + i, ty = goy + j;
396	      if (tx < 10 || tx >= st.w - 10 || ty < 10 || ty >= st.h - 10 || !mact[mi]) continue;
397	      const ti = st.idx(tx, ty);
398	      const gnum = Math.sin((goy + j) * 0.4) * 0.7 + 1.2;
399	      const gnum2 = 0.2 + 0.5 / Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, pres[mi]! - res[mi]!));
400	      if (Math.max(1 - Math.max(0, gnum * gnum2), pres[mi]! / 15) > 0.35 + (solidTileAt(st, tx, ty) ? 0 : 0.5)) {
401	        st.type[ti] = isOreType(st.type[ti]) ? st.type[ti] : GRANITE;
402	        st.flags[ti] = 1; st.liquid[ti] = 0; st.liquidType[ti] = 0;
403	        st.half[ti] = 0; st.slope[ti] = 0;           // ResetToType 清 sTileHeader
404	        st.wall[ti] = 180;
405	      } else if (res[mi]! < 0.01) {
406	        st.flags[ti] = 0;                            // ClearTile：type/wall/液体保留
407	        st.half[ti] = 0; st.slope[ti] = 0;           // ClearSlope
408	        st.wall[ti] = 180;
409	      }
410	      if (st.liquid[ti] > 0 && useLava) st.liquidType[ti] = 2;
411	    }
412	  }
413	  // CleanupTiles：3×3 实心邻 <3 的活性花岗岩清除
414	  const clean: Array<[number, number]> = [];
415	  for (let i = x3; i <= x4; i++) {
416	    for (let j = y3; j <= y4; j++) {
417	      if (!mact[i * 200 + j]) continue;
418	      const tx = gox + i, ty = goy + j;
419	      if (tx < 10 || tx >= st.w - 10 || ty < 10 || ty >= st.h - 10 || !solidTileAt(st, tx, ty)) continue;
420	      let cnt = 0;
421	      for (let a = -1; a <= 1; a++) for (let b = -1; b <= 1; b++) if (solidTileAt(st, tx + a, ty + b)) cnt++;
422	      if (cnt < 3) clean.push([tx, ty]);
423	    }
424	  }
425	  for (const [cx, cy] of clean) {
426	    const ti = st.idx(cx, cy);
427	    st.flags[ti] = 0; st.wall[ti] = 180;
428	    st.half[ti] = 0; st.slope[ti] = 0;               // ClearTile→ClearSlope
429	  }
430	  // PlaceDecorations：FastRandom(seed).WithModifier(65440) 独立流（与 genRand 无关）
431	  const fr = new FastRandom(gs.seed).withModifier(65440);
432	  for (let i = x3; i <= x4; i++) {
433	    for (let j = y3; j <= y4; j++) {
434	      const mi = i * 200 + j;
435	      const tx = gox + i, ty = goy + j;
436	      if (tx < 10 || tx >= st.w - 10 || ty < 10 || ty >= st.h - 10 || !mact[mi]) continue;
437	      // WorldUtils.TileFrame → 165 格 CheckStalactite 重查（GraniteBiome.cs:345）
438	      frame165(st, rng, tx, ty);
439	      // SquareWallFrame：非零墙掷 1×Next(0,3)（GraniteBiome.cs:346 每活性格）
440	      squareWallFrameRoll(st, rng, tx, ty);
441	      const fr2 = fr.withModifierXY(tx, ty);
442	      if (fr2.nextMax(8) === 0 && st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)]) {
443	        if (ty + 1 < st.h && !st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty + 1)]) {
444	          placeStalactiteWithRolls(st, tx, ty + 1, fr2.nextMax(2) === 0, fr2.nextMax(3));
445	        }
446	        if (ty - 1 >= 0 && !st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty - 1)]) {
447	          placeStalactiteWithRolls(st, tx, ty - 1, fr2.nextMax(2) === 0, fr2.nextMax(3));
448	        }
449	      }
450	      if (fr2.nextMax(2) === 0) tileSmoothSlope(st, tx, ty);  // Tile.SmoothSlope（GraniteBiome.cs:352）
451	    }
452	  }
453	}
454	

```

113	function compareStore(st: TileStore, label: string, dumpIdx: number, note?: string, frame?: string): CmpRes | null {

130	    let tJ = jT[i];

132	    const tV = vT[i];

154	    regs[Math.min(41, (x / 100) | 0) + Math.min(11, (y / 100) | 0) * 42]++;

163	    ghostTyp: ghost, unknownSheet: unk,
164	    pairs: top(pairs, 12), newPairs: top(newPairs, 12), actPairs: top(actPairs, 4),
165	    regions: regTop, samples, note, frame,

241	        const rec = LABEL2VAN[label];
242	        eventLog.push({ n, kind: 'INT', label: `${label}${rec ? '#' + rec.idx : ''}`, frame });

253	      console.log('HOOK-EX', String(e).slice(0, 200));
254	    }
255	  };
256	  (RNG.prototype as { reseed: unknown }).reseed = onReseed;
257	
258	  try {
259	    await generateWorld({
260	      width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,
261	      onWorldPartial: (w, _i, name) => { store = w.store; completedSlot = name; slotCount++; },
262	    });
263	    if (store) {

267	  } catch (e) {
268	    console.log('GEN-EX(部分结果保留):', String(e).slice(0, 300));
269	  } finally {
270	    (RNG.prototype as { reseed: unknown }).reseed = origReseed;
271	  }
272	
273	  const out = {
274	    meta: { seed: 9293480, evil: 0, w: W, h: H, dumpDir: VG, slots: slotCount, events: evN, compares: results.length, unknownSlots },
275	    results,
276	    eventLog,
277	  };
278	  try { writeFileSync('/tmp/sw-contentparity.json', JSON.stringify(out)); } catch (e) { console.log('JSON写失败', String(e)); }
279	
280	  const zero = results.filter((r2) => r2.typ + r2.act + r2.wal + r2.liq === 0);
281	  const diffs = results.filter((r2) => r2.typ + r2.act + r2.wal + r2.liq > 0)
282	    .sort((a, b) => (a.dumpIdx - b.dumpIdx));
283	  console.log('\n==== 全部比对边界（按 dump 序）====');
284	  for (const d of diffs) {
285	    console.log(`[${String(d.dumpIdx).padStart(3, '0')}] ${d.dumpName.padEnd(30)} ${d.label.padEnd(26)} typ=${String(d.typ).padStart(7)}(实${String(d.typ - d.ghostTyp).padStart(7)}/ghost${String(d.ghostTyp).padStart(6)}) act=${String(d.act).padStart(7)} wal=${String(d.wal).padStart(7)} liq=${String(d.liq).padStart(6)} | new=${d.newTyp}/${d.newAct}/${d.newWal}/${d.newLiq}`);
286	  }
287	  console.log(`\n==== 零差异（${zero.length}/${results.length}）====`);
288	  console.log(zero.map((r2) => `${String(r2.dumpIdx).padStart(3, '0')}_${r2.dumpName}`).join('  '));
289	  console.log(`\nslots=${slotCount} events=${evN} compares=${results.length} unknownSlots=${JSON.stringify(unknownSlots)}`);
290	  console.log('槽序(as-run):');
291	  const slotSeq = eventLog.filter((e) => e.kind === 'HEAD').map((e) => e.label.replace('head(after:', '').replace(')', ''));
292	  console.log('  ' + slotSeq.join(' → '));
293	  console.log('JSON: /tmp/sw-contentparity.json');
294	}, 480000);

~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/tools/_x52slope.test.ts
// 一次性(X 批):全图@52 残余幻影坡溯源——首写事件 + 该格 type/wall + @52 时点态。
// SW_PARITY=1 npx vitest run tools/_x52slope.test.ts
import { it } from 'vitest';
const RUN = !!process.env.SW_PARITY;
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';
import type { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';

const W = 4200, H = 1200, N = W * H;
const TV = '/tmp/sw-hsv';
const rd = (p: string) => new Uint8Array(readFileSync(p).buffer.slice(0));
const t52h = rd(`${TV}/hs52.half`), t52s = rd(`${TV}/hs52.slope`);

let store: TileStore | null = null;
let completedSlot: string | null = null;
const labels: string[] = [];
// 每格 slope/half 非零首现事件下标(255=从未)
let firstEv: Uint8Array | null = null;
// @52 时点快照(全通道关键项)
let snap52: { s: Uint8Array; h: Uint8Array; t: Uint16Array; a: Uint8Array; w: Uint16Array } | null = null;

(RUN ? it : it.skip)('x52 whole-map phantom provenance', async () => {
  const origReseed = RNG.prototype.reseed;
  const onReseed = function (this: RNG, seed: number) {
    origReseed.call(this, seed);
    try {
      const stackLine = (new Error().stack ?? '').split('\n')
        .find((l) => l.includes('/src/') && !l.includes('rng.ts')) ?? '?';
      const mFn = /at\s+(?:async\s+)?(\S+)\s+\(/.exec(stackLine);
      const func = mFn ? mFn[1] : '?';
      let label: string;
      if (func.includes('generateWorld')) {
        label = `head:${completedSlot ?? 'start'}`;
      } else {
        const mLoc = /\(.*(src\/[^\s?]+):(\d+):\d+\)/.exec(stackLine);
        label = '?';
        if (mLoc) {
          const rel = mLoc[1]!.slice(mLoc[1]!.indexOf('src/'));
          const src = readFileSync(`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/${rel}`, 'utf8').split('\n');
          for (let k = +mLoc[2]; k >= Math.max(0, +mLoc[2] - 2); k--) {
            const cm = /\/\/\s*▶([^\s(（]+)/.exec(src[k - 1] ?? '');
            if (cm) { label = cm[1]!; break; }
          }
        }
      }
      const st = store;
      if (!st) return;
      const ev = labels.length;
      if (ev >= 250) return;
      labels.push(label);
      if (label === 'head:海滩装饰' && !snap52) {
        snap52 = {
          s: st.slope.slice(), h: st.half.slice(), t: st.type.slice(),
          a: st.flags.slice(), w: st.wall.slice(),
        };
      }
      if (!firstEv) firstEv = new Uint8Array(N).fill(255);
      const fe = firstEv;
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        if (fe[i] === 255 && (st.slope[i] || st.half[i])) fe[i] = ev;
      }
    } catch { /* ignore */ }
  };
  (RNG.prototype as { reseed: unknown }).reseed = onReseed;
  try {
    await generateWorld({
      width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,
      onWorldPartial: (_w, _i, name) => { if (!store) store = _w.store; completedSlot = name; },
    });
  } catch (e) { console.log('GEN-EX:', String(e).slice(0, 300)); } finally {
    (RNG.prototype as { reseed: unknown }).reseed = origReseed;
  }
  if (!snap52 || !firstEv) { console.log('!! no @52 snapshot'); return; }
  const t52t = new Uint16Array(readFileSync(`${TV}/hs52.typ`).buffer.slice(0));
  const t52a = rd(`${TV}/hs52.act`);
  const t52w = new Uint16Array(readFileSync(`${TV}/hs52.wal`).buffer.slice(0));
  const sheetOf = (t: number) => { const s = TILE_DEFS[t]?.vanilla?.sheet; return typeof s === 'number' ? s : -1; };
  let phantom = 0, miss = 0, diffVal = 0;
  const prov = new Map<string, number>();
  const cellsByProv = new Map<string, string[]>();
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const js = snap52.s[i]! * 8 + snap52.h[i]!, tr = t52s[i]! * 8 + t52h[i]!;
    if (js === tr) continue;
    if (js && !tr) {
      phantom++;
      // 该格四通道是否也对不上(区分"纯坡位差"与"更深的 typ 债")
      const jsT = snap52.a[i] ? sheetOf(snap52.t[i]!) : 0;
      const trT = t52a[i] ? t52t[i]! : 0;
      const chOk = jsT === trT && snap52.w[i] === t52w[i];
      const fe = firstEv![i]!;
      // 快照在窗口头 reseed 时拍 → fe=K 的写入者实为 labels[K-1] 窗口(前一 pass)
      const evLabel = fe === 255 ? 'never(残留)' : fe === 0 ? '(init)' : labels[fe - 1]!;
      const key = `${evLabel} | js typ${jsT}${snap52.a[i] ? '' : '!act'} w${snap52.w[i]} | truth typ${trT} ${chOk ? '四通道同' : '★四通道异'}`;
      prov.set(key, (prov.get(key) ?? 0) + 1);
      if ((cellsByProv.get(key) ?? []).length < 6) {
        const arr = cellsByProv.get(key) ?? [];
        arr.push(`(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0})`);
        cellsByProv.set(key, arr);
      }
    }
    else if (!js && tr) miss++;
    else diffVal++;
  }
  console.log(`全图@52: 幻影=${phantom} 漏=${miss} 值差=${diffVal}`);
  for (const [k, v] of [...prov.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])) {
    console.log(`  ${String(v).padStart(4)}  ${k}   ${(cellsByProv.get(k) ?? []).join(' ')}`);
  }
}, 480000);

```

73	                        hT = (hT ^ hi) * 16777619u;

87	                        hi = (uint)(gho >> 8);
88	                        hG = (hG ^ lo) * 16777619u;
89	                        hG = (hG ^ hi) * 16777619u;
90	                        n++;
91	                    }
92	                }

99	                    Terraria.WorldGen.WorldGenParam_Evil + "," + (Terraria.WorldGen.crimson ? 1 : 0) + "|" +

131	        private static void DumpGrids(string passName, int W, int H, Terraria.Tile[,] tile)
132	        {
133	            string dumpAll = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_DUMP_ALL");
134	            if (dumpAll != null)
135	            {
136	                try
137	                {
138	                    StringBuilder sbn = new StringBuilder();
139	                    foreach (char ch in passName)
140	                        sbn.Append(char.IsLetterOrDigit(ch) ? ch : '_');
141	                    WriteGrid(dumpAll + "/" + _idx.ToString("000") + "_" + sbn.ToString(), W, H, tile);
142	                }
143	                catch (Exception e) { Mark("#dumpall-ex " + passName + " " + e.GetType().Name + ": " + e.Message); }
144	                return;
145	            }
146	            string cfg = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_GRID_DUMP");
147	            if (cfg == null) return;
148	            foreach (string one in cfg.Split(','))
149	            {
150	                string[] kv = one.Split(':');
151	                if (kv.Length != 2 || kv[0] != passName) continue;
152	                try
153	                {
154	                    WriteGrid(kv[1], W, H, tile);
155	                    Mark("#griddump " + passName + " -> " + kv[1]);
156	                }
157	                catch (Exception e) { Mark("#griddump-ex " + passName + " " + e.GetType().Name + ": " + e.Message); }
158	            }
159	        }
160	
161	        private static void WriteGrid(string p, int W, int H, Terraria.Tile[,] tile)
162	        {
163	            int n = W * H;
164	            byte[] typ = new byte[n * 2];
165	            byte[] act = new byte[n];
166	            byte[] wal = new byte[n * 2];
167	            byte[] liq = new byte[n];
168	            int i = 0;
169	            for (int y = 0; y < H; y++)
170	                for (int x = 0; x < W; x++, i++)
171	                {
172	                    Terraria.Tile t = tile[x, y];
173	                    ushort ty2 = t.type;
174	                    typ[i * 2] = (byte)(ty2 & 0xFF);
175	                    typ[i * 2 + 1] = (byte)(ty2 >> 8);
176	                    act[i] = t.active() ? (byte)1 : (byte)0;
177	                    ushort wv = t.wall;
178	                    wal[i * 2] = (byte)(wv & 0xFF);
179	                    wal[i * 2 + 1] = (byte)(wv >> 8);
180	                    liq[i] = t.liquid;
181	                }
182	            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".typ", typ);
183	            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".act", act);
184	            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".wal", wal);
185	            File.WriteAllBytes(p + ".liq", liq);
186	        }
187	
188	        private static string StreamHash()
189	        {
190	            try
191	            {
192	                object r = Terraria.Main.rand;
193	                if (r == null) return "norand";
194	                if (_fiSeedArray == null)
195	                {
196	                    Type t = r.GetType();
197	                    _fiSeedArray = t.GetField("SeedArray", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
198	                    _fiInext = t.GetField("inext", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);
199	                }
200	                int[] sa = (int[])_fiSeedArray.GetValue(r);
201	                uint ix = (uint)_fiInext.GetValue(r);
202	                uint h = 2166136261u;
203	                for (int k = 0; k < sa.Length; k++)
204	                {
205	                    int v = sa[k];
206	                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
207	                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 8) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
208	                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 16) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
209	                    h = (h ^ (uint)((v >> 24) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
210	                }
211	                h = (h ^ (ix & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
212	                h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> 8) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
213	                h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> 16) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
214	                h = (h ^ (uint)((ix >> 24) & 0xFF)) * 16777619u;
215	                return Hex(h);
216	            }
217	            catch (Exception e) { return "ex:" + e.GetType().Name; }
218	        }
219	
220	        private static void Mark(string s)
221	        {
222	            try { File.AppendAllText("/tmp/sw-num4/home/passchain_err.txt", s + "\n"); } catch { }
223	        }
224	
225	        // ===== rolltrace（marble/granite 逐列掷骰轨迹,2026-08-16）=====
226	        public static long RollCount;
227	        public static bool RollTraceOn;
228	        private static System.IO.StreamWriter _rt;
229	        public static void RollTraceBegin()
230	        {
231	            try
232	            {
233	                string path = System.Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("SW_ROLLTRACE");
234	                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path)) return;

236	                _rt = new System.IO.StreamWriter(path, false);
237	                RollTraceOn = true;
238	                _rt.WriteLine("#rolltrace begin");
239	                _rt.Flush();
240	            }
241	            catch { }
242	        }
243	        public static void RollBump() { if (RollTraceOn) RollCount++; }
244	        public static void SlabEnter(string who)
245	        {
246	            if (!RollTraceOn || _rt == null) return;
247	            try { _rt.WriteLine(who + "|" + RollCount); _rt.Flush(); } catch { }
248	        }
249	        public static void SlabEnterXY(int x, int y)
250	        {
251	            if (!RollTraceOn || _rt == null) return;
252	            try { _rt.WriteLine("MX|" + x + "," + y + "|" + RollCount); _rt.Flush(); } catch { }
253	        }
254	        public static void TightEnter(int x, int y)
255	        {
256	            if (!RollTraceOn || _rt == null) return;
257	            try { _rt.WriteLine("PT|" + x + "," + y + "|" + RollCount); _rt.Flush(); } catch { }
258	        }
259	        // ===== slope 转储：biome2 slab 70..78 的 slab 区邻域 half/slope/type/active =====
260	        private static int _mxInBiome;
261	        private static int _biomeWithSlabs;
262	        public static void SlabDumpXY(int x, int y)
263	        {

265	            _mxInBiome++;
266	            if (_mxInBiome > 78) return;
267	            try

269	                var sb = new StringBuilder();

271	                for (int dy = -6; dy <= 8; dy++)
272	                {
273	                    for (int dx = -3; dx <= 7; dx++)

275	                        var t = Terraria.Main.tile[x + dx, y + dy];

277	                        if (!t.active()) { sb.Append(t.type == 0 ? '.' : 'g'); continue; }
278	                        int sl = t.slope(); bool hb = t.halfBrick();
279	                        sb.Append(sl > 0 ? (char)('0' + sl) : (hb ? 'h' : (t.type == 367 ? 'M' : (t.type == 165 ? 'S' : 'o'))));

        void Hook(TypeDefinition t, string method, string tag)
        {
            var m = t.Methods.First(x => x.Name == method) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException(t.FullName + "::" + method);
            var il = m.Body.GetILProcessor();
            var anchor = m.Body.Instructions[0];   // 锚定首指令——Instructions[0] 会随插入漂移
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldstr, tag));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Call, slabEnter));

            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldstr, "MP"));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Call, slabEnter));

            var m = marble.Methods.First(x => x.Name == "PlaceSlab");

            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldarg_2));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldarg_3));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Call, slabXY));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldarg_2));

            Console.WriteLine("[inject] MarbleBiome::PlaceSlab <- SlabEnterXY+SlabDumpXY(x, y)");
        }
        {
            var pt = worldGen.Methods.First(x => x.Name == "PlaceTight" && x.IsStatic);
            var il = pt.Body.GetILProcessor();
            var anchor = pt.Body.Instructions[0];
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldarg_0));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Ldarg_1));
            il.InsertBefore(anchor, il.Create(OpCodes.Call, tightXY));
            Console.WriteLine("[inject] WorldGen::PlaceTight <- TightEnter(x, y)");
        }
        Hook(granite, "Place", "GP");
        Hook(granite, "PlaceDecorations", "G");
        asm.Write(outPath);
        Console.WriteLine($"[rolltrace] written {outPath}");
        break;

// 确定性随机数：Terraria 1.4.5.6 位级对齐。
//
// ── UnifiedRandom（Terraria.Utilities/UnifiedRandom.cs 1:1）─────────────────
// .NET System.Random 的 Knuth 减法生成器（MSEED=161803398），纯 int32 运算。
// 位级移植要点（JS/C# 数值差异全部钉死，改动前先读）：
//  1. C# int 溢出 unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS：本实现所有中间值都落在 int32 范围内
//     （diff 最小 -(2^31-1)，`+((n>>31)&0x7FFFFFFF)` 归位后 ∈[0,2^31-1]），
//     无需模拟回绕；存储用 Int32Array 与 C# int[] 同构。
//  2. `(int)double` 是向零截断 ↔ JS Math.trunc（Sample()≥0 时与 floor 等价，
//     但必须写 trunc 以防有人把 internalSample 改出负值）。
//  3. `Sample()` 常数 4.656612875245797E-10 = 1/(2^31-1)，十进制字面量两语言
//     解析为同一 double，乘法 IEEE754 位级一致。
//  4. `Next(min,max)` 的 range 用 long（≤2^32）↔ JS number 精确整数，安全。
//     大 range 分支的 `(int)(long+min)` unchecked 回绕 ↔ JS `|0`（ToInt32 mod 2^32）。
//  5. `GetSampleForLargeRange` 的 `% 2 == 0`：C# 余数带符号但只判零，JS `%` 同判。
//  金标：tests/golden/unified-random.json 由真·Terraria.exe 反射导出
//  （tools/golden/rand-golden.cs），tests/unified-random.test.ts 位级断言。
//
// ── 种子解析（WorldFileData.TranslateSeed, cs:399-410 1:1）────────────────
// 数字字符串 → Math.Abs(int)（int.MinValue→int.MaxValue）；非数字 →
// ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32.Calculate（IEEE 表，按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符，
// 注意 `(crc ^ c) & 0xFF` 只取 char 低字节）。Crc32 结果可为负，直接作种子。
export class UnifiedRandom {
  private seedArray: Int32Array;
  private inext = 0;

  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.seedArray = new Int32Array(56);
    this.setSeed(seed);
  }

  /** C# SetSeed（UnifiedRandom.cs:25-61）1:1 */
  setSeed(seed: number): void {
    this.seedArray.fill(0);
    // C#: (Seed == int.MinValue) ? int.MaxValue : Math.Abs(Seed)
    const num = seed === -2147483648 ? 2147483647 : Math.abs(seed);
    let num2 = 161803398 - num;
    this.seedArray[55] = num2;
    let num3 = 1;
    for (let j = 1; j < 55; j++) {
      const num4 = (21 * j) % 55;
      this.seedArray[num4] = num3;
      num3 = (num2 - num3) | 0; // C# unchecked int：可能回绕，|0 = ToInt32 同语义
      if (num3 < 0) num3 += 2147483647; // C# += int.MaxValue（不是 2^32！）
      num2 = this.seedArray[num4];
    }
    for (let k = 1; k < 5; k++) {
      for (let l = 1; l < 56; l++) {
        this.seedArray[l] -= this.seedArray[1 + ((l + 30) % 55)];
        if (this.seedArray[l] < 0) this.seedArray[l] += 2147483647;
      }
    }
    this.inext = 0;
  }

  /** C# Sample（:63-65）*/
  private sample(): number {
    return this.internalSample() * 4.656612875245797e-10;
  }

  /** C# InternalSample（:67-86）1:1。inext 为 uint 语义（0..55，无溢出面）*/
  private internalSample(): number {
    let num = this.inext + 1;
    if (num > 55) num = 1;
    let num2 = num + 21;
    if (num2 > 55) num2 -= 55;
    let num3 = this.seedArray[num] - this.seedArray[num2];
    if (num3 === 2147483647) num3--;
    num3 = num3 + ((num3 >> 31) & 0x7fffffff); // >>31 算术移位，JS/C# 一致
    this.seedArray[num] = num3;
    this.inext = num;
    return num3;
  }

  /** C# GetSampleForLargeRange（:96-104）。注意两次 InternalSample 的求值顺序 */
  private getSampleForLargeRange(): number {
    let num = this.internalSample();
    if (this.internalSample() % 2 === 0) num = -num;
    return (num + 2147483646) / 4294967293;
  }

        int at = -1;
        for (int k = 0; k < body.Instructions.Count - 1; k++)

var rp = new ReaderParameters { AssemblyResolver = resolver, ReadSymbols = false };
using var asm = AssemblyDefinition.ReadAssembly(exe, rp);
var mod = asm.MainModule;
var terrainPass = mod.Types.FirstOrDefault(t => t.FullName == "Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.TerrainPass")

#!/bin/bash
# MonoKickstart Shell Script
# Written by Ethan "flibitijibibo" Lee

cd "`dirname "$0"`"

UNAME=`uname`
ARCH=`uname -m`

if [ "$UNAME" == "Darwin" ]; then
	export DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH=./osx/:$DYLD_LIBRARY_PATH

	if [ "$STEAM_DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES" != "" ] && [ "$DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES" == "" ]; then
		export DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES="$STEAM_DYLD_INSERT_LIBRARIES"
	fi
	
	if [ "$SteamClientLaunch" = "1" ] && [ "$ARCH" = "x86_64" ] && [ "$(sysctl -n sysctl.proc_translated 2>/dev/null || echo 0)" = "1" ]; then
		arch -arm64 ./TerrariaServer.bin.osx $@
	else
		./TerrariaServer.bin.osx $@
	fi
fi

- `_d24iso.test.ts` VN 表 `gra: 781137` 多抄一位：独立复算（`/tmp/sw-ycal/nfind.mjs`，UR 状态+StreamHash 同构直算 0..1.6M 全扫）granite 末态 `a86ee76c` **唯一命中 N=78,137**，781137 不命中；marble `74c514f7` 命中 248,340 与 J 批一致（算法互证）。78,134−781,137=−703,003 分毫吻合——悬案值即笔误出生证。真差仅 **3 掷**。

**② -3 掷与 7 格残余 = 隔离重放缺 slope/half 输入的伪影**
- 新真值资产 `/tmp/sw-ycal/van22`（106 pass×8 通道，x86 全链重采，022 四通道 vs van_x86_g、052 slope/half vs sw-hsv 双 SAME 复核）。
- A/B 实验（`_yiso22.test.ts`）：不注入坡位→78134(-3)+7 格残余（biome5 (2366,766) 区）；**注入→draws 78,137 精确+四通道 0 差+输出 slope/half 也 0 差**。根因=CA 初值 `res=SolidTile?4:1` 坡面敏感。per-biome 对拍：唯 b5 差 3，b1-b4/b6-b8 全精确。**granite pass 本体 1:1 无瑕疵**。

**①+② @52 残余 219 格归属定谳 → 全部移交 DungeonPass（#32）**
- `tools/_ypipe22.test.ts`：JS 管线 @22 全图 **slope/half=0 差**（typ=39 为 [019] 起继承幽灵土）——Marble/Granite 坡位写入与真值逐格一致，82 纯坡位格 @22 时真值同坡。
- “花岗岩区”勘正：x≈519-586,y≈797-862 真身是**地牢**（真值粉砖 44×3060+裂砖 483×293），首群系恰好落位其中。[031] 干净→[032] Dungeon 边界 new=2330/1328/4/0：债全由 #32 引入（真值砖写 ResetToType 清坡，JS 不清坡+砖型债 44↔91↔241↔240↔42）。**DungeonPass.ts 属禁区（他人在改 [032]），证据链已在文档移交**：砖写入补清 half/slope（同 X 批 TemplePass.setBrick 法）后 219 格预期随 [032] 塌缩归零。

**④ rig 复验**：管线 [021]/[022]/[023] **new=0/0/0/0 维持**（[019]-[031] 无新增）；隔离五 pass（DirtToMud/Silt/Islands/Marble/Granite）掷数+四通道**首次全精确全绿**。

**变更**：`tools/_d24iso.test.ts`（VN 勘正+真值坡位回注固化）；新增 `_ygran/_yiso22/_ypipe22.test.ts`；`MarbleGranitePass.ts` 仅加休眠探针 `__swGranitePlace`（同 __swSlabProbe 惯例，2 行）；ShimmerPass/金标/禁区六文件零改动；探针 server 已杀净（并行会话实例未动）；oracle 清单追加 N 勘正+granite 检查点须含真值坡位输入两条。
